+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,

+                }
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

codex
The packed/cache-blocked kernel now sustains roughly 23–26 GFLOP/s on one core and improves the 2048² four-thread time from about 730 ms to 211 ms, with all tested sizes still validating. The higher-thread runs are now dominated by NUMA placement: standard `std::vector(size)` zero-fills all pages on the launching core before OpenMP can distribute them. I’m removing that hidden serial first-touch using C++20’s uninitialized array allocation, then letting the existing parallel initialization/computation place pages on the worker nodes; result formatting remains byte-for-byte equivalent by materializing a vector only after timing when `-r` is requested.
apply patch

+namespace {

+// Four rows reuse every cache line fetched from B four times.  The column
+// width gives the compiler eight independent vector accumulators, which is
+// enough to hide FMA latency without spilling registers.
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;
+constexpr size_t rowCacheBlock = 16;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 16;
+#else
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 8;
+#endif
+
+inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                             double* const C, const size_t N,
+                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
+    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum01 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum10 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum11 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum20 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum21 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum30 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum31 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        __m512d b0 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
+        __m512d b1 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 8);
+        // Keep these shared operands in registers.  Without this constraint,
+        // some compilers fold the same load into all four row FMAs.
+        __asm__ volatile("" : "+v"(b0), "+v"(b1));
+        const __m512d a0 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 0) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a1 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 1) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a2 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 2) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a3 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 3) * N + k]);
+
+        sum00 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 8, sum01);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 8, sum11);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 8, sum21);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 8, sum31);
+#elif defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
+        __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 4);
+        __asm__ volatile("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 0) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 1) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 2) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 3) * N + k);
+
+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
+#else
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
+    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
+        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            const double bValue = b[j];
+            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
+            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
+            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
+                                const size_t column,
+                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
+#else
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
+    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
+        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            const double bValue = b[j];
+            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
+            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
+            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
+                                const size_t column,
+                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;

+#else

+inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                             double* const C, const size_t N,
+                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
+    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum01 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum10 = _mm512_setzero_pd();

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(__i386__)
#include <immintrin.h>
#endif

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(double* const mat, const size_t N) {
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

namespace {

// Four rows reuse every cache line fetched from B four times.  The column
// width gives the compiler eight independent vector accumulators, which is
// enough to hide FMA latency without spilling registers.
constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;
constexpr size_t rowCacheBlock = 16;
#if defined(__AVX512F__)
constexpr size_t columnBlock = 16;
#else
constexpr size_t columnBlock = 8;
#endif

inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
                             double* const C, const size_t N,
                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
#if defined(__AVX512F__)
    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum01 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum10 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum11 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum20 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum21 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum30 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    __m512d sum31 = _mm512_setzero_pd();

    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
        __m512d b0 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
        __m512d b1 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 8);
        // Keep these shared operands in registers.  Without this constraint,
        // some compilers fold the same load into all four row FMAs.
        __asm__ volatile("" : "+v"(b0), "+v"(b1));
        const __m512d a0 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 0) * N + k]);
        const __m512d a1 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 1) * N + k]);
        const __m512d a2 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 2) * N + k]);
        const __m512d a3 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 3) * N + k]);

        sum00 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
        sum01 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
        sum10 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
        sum11 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
        sum20 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
        sum21 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
        sum30 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
        sum31 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
    }

    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 8, sum01);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 8, sum11);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 8, sum21);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 8, sum31);
#elif defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
        __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
        __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 4);
        __asm__ volatile("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 0) * N + k);
        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 1) * N + k);
        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 2) * N + k);
        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 3) * N + k);

        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
    }

    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
#else
    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};

    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];

#pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
            const double bValue = b[j];
            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
        }
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
#pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
        }
    }
#endif
}

inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
                                double* const C, const size_t N,
                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
                                const size_t column,
                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};

    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
#pragma omp simd
            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
            }
        }
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
#pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
        }
    }
}

}  // namespace

void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);

    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
#pragma omp parallel
    {
#pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
            double* const destination =
                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
#pragma omp simd
                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
                        b[k * N + j + offset];
                }
            }
        }

        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
            const size_t rowEnd =
                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
                }
            }
            if (fullColumns < N) {
                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
                }
            }
        }

        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
#pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
            }
            if (fullColumns < N) {
                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
                                    N - fullColumns);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const double* const A, const double* const B,
                    const double* const C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Leave pages untouched until the OpenMP workers initialize or compute
    // them.  This first-touch placement avoids pinning all large matrices to
    // the NUMA node on which the main thread happened to allocate them.
    const size_t elementCount = N * N;
    auto A = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(elementCount);
    auto B = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(elementCount);
    auto C = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(elementCount);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A.get(), N);
    initMatrix(B.get(), N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A.get(), B.get(), C.get(), N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        std::vector<double> result;
        if (elementCount != 0) {
            result.assign(C.get(), C.get() + elementCount);
        }
        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A.get(), B.get(), C.get(), N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 0) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 1) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 2) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 3) * N + k);
+
+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
+#else
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
+    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
+        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            const double bValue = b[j];
+            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
+            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
+            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
+                                const size_t column,
+                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;

+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 8;

+inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                             double* const C, const size_t N,
+                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
+    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();

+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,

+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    const size_t threadCount = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    const size_t rowCacheBlock =
+        fullRows / maxRowCacheBlock >= threadCount
+            ? maxRowCacheBlock
+            : (fullRows / (2 * rowBlock) >= threadCount ? 2 * rowBlock
+                                                         : rowBlock);
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];

+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
             }

+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
+            }
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+namespace {
+
+// Four rows reuse every cache line fetched from B four times.  The column
+// width gives the compiler eight independent vector accumulators, which is
+// enough to hide FMA latency without spilling registers.
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;
+constexpr size_t maxRowCacheBlock = 16;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 16;
+#else
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 8;
+#endif
+
+inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                             double* const C, const size_t N,
+                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
+    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum01 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum10 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum11 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum20 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum21 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum30 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum31 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        __m512d b0 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
+        __m512d b1 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 8);
+        // Keep these shared operands in registers.  Without this constraint,
+        // some compilers fold the same load into all four row FMAs.
+        __asm__ volatile("" : "+v"(b0), "+v"(b1));
+        const __m512d a0 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 0) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a1 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 1) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a2 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 2) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a3 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 3) * N + k]);
+
+        sum00 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 8, sum01);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 8, sum11);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 8, sum21);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 8, sum31);
+#elif defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
+        __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 4);
+        __asm__ volatile("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 0) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 1) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 2) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 3) * N + k);
+
+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
+#else
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
+    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
+        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            const double bValue = b[j];
+            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
+            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
+            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
+                                const size_t column,
+                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    const size_t threadCount = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    const size_t rowCacheBlock =
+        fullRows / maxRowCacheBlock >= threadCount
+            ? maxRowCacheBlock
+            : (fullRows / (2 * rowBlock) >= threadCount ? 2 * rowBlock
+                                                         : rowBlock);
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
+            }
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 8, sum21);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 8, sum31);
+#elif defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
+        __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 4);
+        __asm__ volatile("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 0) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 1) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 2) * N + k);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 3) * N + k);
+
+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
+#else
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
+    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
+        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];

+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            const double bValue = b[j];
+            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
+            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
+            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
+                                const size_t column,
+                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    const size_t threadCount = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    const size_t rowCacheBlock =
+        fullRows / maxRowCacheBlock >= threadCount
+            ? maxRowCacheBlock
+            : (fullRows / (2 * rowBlock) >= threadCount ? 2 * rowBlock
+                                                         : rowBlock);
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
+            }
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

codex
NUMA-aware first-touch materially restored multicore scaling: with threads spread across cores, the 2048² run scaled from about 757 ms on one thread to 70 ms on 16, 41 ms on 32, and 22–26 ms on 64–128 threads. I’ve also made the cache slab size adapt to the requested OpenMP team so smaller matrices expose enough independent work. The final pass is checking a fresh configure, runtime OpenMP linkage, CLI compatibility, and deterministic result summaries.

+namespace {
+
+// Four rows reuse every cache line fetched from B four times.  The column
+// width gives the compiler eight independent vector accumulators, which is
+// enough to hide FMA latency without spilling registers.
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;
+constexpr size_t maxRowCacheBlock = 16;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 16;
+#else
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 8;
+#endif
+
+inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                             double* const C, const size_t N,
+                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
+    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum01 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum10 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum11 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum20 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum21 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum30 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+    __m512d sum31 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        __m512d b0 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
+        __m512d b1 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 8);
+        // Keep these shared operands in registers.  Without this constraint,
+        // some compilers fold the same load into all four row FMAs.
+        __asm__ volatile("" : "+v"(b0), "+v"(b1));
+        const __m512d a0 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 0) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a1 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 1) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a2 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 2) * N + k]);
+        const __m512d a3 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 3) * N + k]);
+

+        sum01 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        sum10 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);

+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

+#else
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};

+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot

+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }

+inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                                double* const C, const size_t N,
+                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
+                                const size_t column,
+                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
+    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
+        }
+    }
+}

+}  // namespace

+void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
+                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
+    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
+    const size_t threadCount = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    const size_t rowCacheBlock =
+        fullRows / maxRowCacheBlock >= threadCount
+            ? maxRowCacheBlock
+            : (fullRows / (2 * rowBlock) >= threadCount ? 2 * rowBlock
+                                                         : rowBlock);
+    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
+
+    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
+    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
+    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+            double* const destination =
+                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
+                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
+                        b[k * N + j + offset];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
+        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
+        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
+        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
+             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
+            const size_t rowEnd =
+                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
+                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
+                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
+                }
+            }
         }
+
+        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
+        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
+            }
+            if (fullColumns < N) {
+                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
+                                    N - fullColumns);
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

    17	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
    18	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    19	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);

    22	void initMatrix(double* const mat, const size_t N) {
    23	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    24	    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
    25	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    26	            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
    27	        }
    28	    }
    29	}

    33	// Four rows reuse every cache line fetched from B four times.  The column
    34	// width gives the compiler eight independent vector accumulators, which is
    35	// enough to hide FMA latency without spilling registers.
    36	constexpr size_t rowBlock = 4;
    37	constexpr size_t maxRowCacheBlock = 16;
    38	#if defined(__AVX512F__)
    39	constexpr size_t columnBlock = 16;
    40	#else
    41	constexpr size_t columnBlock = 8;
    42	#endif
    43	
    44	inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
    45	                             double* const C, const size_t N,
    46	                             const size_t row, const size_t column) noexcept {
    47	    const double* const bPanel = B + (column / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
    48	#if defined(__AVX512F__)
    49	    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    50	    __m512d sum01 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    51	    __m512d sum10 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    52	    __m512d sum11 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    53	    __m512d sum20 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    54	    __m512d sum21 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    55	    __m512d sum30 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    56	    __m512d sum31 = _mm512_setzero_pd();
    57	
    58	    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    59	        __m512d b0 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
    60	        __m512d b1 = _mm512_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 8);
    61	        // Keep these shared operands in registers.  Without this constraint,
    62	        // some compilers fold the same load into all four row FMAs.
    63	        __asm__ volatile("" : "+v"(b0), "+v"(b1));
    64	        const __m512d a0 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 0) * N + k]);
    65	        const __m512d a1 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 1) * N + k]);
    66	        const __m512d a2 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 2) * N + k]);
    67	        const __m512d a3 = _mm512_set1_pd(A[(row + 3) * N + k]);
    68	
    69	        sum00 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
    70	        sum01 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
    71	        sum10 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
    72	        sum11 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
    73	        sum20 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
    74	        sum21 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
    75	        sum30 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
    76	        sum31 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
    80	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 8, sum01);
    81	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
    82	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 8, sum11);
    83	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
    84	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 8, sum21);
    85	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
    86	    _mm512_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 8, sum31);
    87	#elif defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
    88	    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    89	    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    90	    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    91	    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    92	    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    93	    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    94	    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    95	    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    96	
    97	    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    98	        __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock);
    99	        __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bPanel + k * columnBlock + 4);
   100	        __asm__ volatile("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
   101	        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 0) * N + k);
   102	        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 1) * N + k);
   103	        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 2) * N + k);
   104	        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(A + (row + 3) * N + k);
   105	
   106	        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
   107	        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
   108	        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
   109	        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
   110	        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
   111	        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
   112	        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
   113	        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column, sum00);
   117	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 0) * N + column + 4, sum01);
   118	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column, sum10);
   119	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);
   120	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column, sum20);
   121	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 2) * N + column + 4, sum21);
   122	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column, sum30);
   123	    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);
   124	#else
   125	    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
   126	
   127	    // k remains strictly increasing, as in the original implementation.
   128	    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot
   129	    // product and results remain deterministic for every OpenMP thread count.
   130	    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   131	        const double* const b = bPanel + k * columnBlock;
   132	        const double a0 = A[(row + 0) * N + k];
   133	        const double a1 = A[(row + 1) * N + k];
   134	        const double a2 = A[(row + 2) * N + k];
   135	        const double a3 = A[(row + 3) * N + k];
   136	
   137	#pragma omp simd
   138	        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
   139	            const double bValue = b[j];
   140	            sums[0][j] += a0 * bValue;
   141	            sums[1][j] += a1 * bValue;
   142	            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
   143	            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
   144	        }
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
   148	#pragma omp simd
   149	        for (size_t j = 0; j < columnBlock; ++j) {
   150	            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
   151	        }
   152	    }
   153	#endif
   154	}
   155	
   156	inline void multiplyPartialTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
   157	                                double* const C, const size_t N,
   158	                                const size_t row, const size_t rows,
   159	                                const size_t column,
   160	                                const size_t columns) noexcept {
   161	    alignas(64) double sums[rowBlock][columnBlock] = {};
   162	
   163	    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   164	        const double* const b = B + k * N + column;
   165	        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
   166	            const double aValue = A[(row + i) * N + k];
   167	#pragma omp simd
   168	            for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
   169	                sums[i][j] += aValue * b[j];
   170	            }
   171	        }
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
   175	#pragma omp simd
   176	        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
   177	            C[(row + i) * N + column + j] = sums[i][j];
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	}
   181	
   182	}  // namespace

   184	void matrixMultiply(const double* const a, const double* const b,
   185	                    double* const c, const size_t N) {
   186	    const size_t fullRows = N - N % rowBlock;
   187	    const size_t fullColumns = N - N % columnBlock;
   188	    const size_t threadCount = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
   189	    const size_t rowCacheBlock =
   190	        fullRows / maxRowCacheBlock >= threadCount
   191	            ? maxRowCacheBlock
   192	            : (fullRows / (2 * rowBlock) >= threadCount ? 2 * rowBlock
   193	                                                         : rowBlock);
   194	    auto packedB = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(N * fullColumns);
   195	
   196	    // One persistent team packs B and computes C without repeatedly entering
   197	    // parallel regions.  Panel-major packing makes the hot kernel's B reads
   198	    // contiguous while preserving the original values and arithmetic order.
   199	#pragma omp parallel
   200	    {
   201	#pragma omp for schedule(static)
   202	        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
   203	            double* const destination =
   204	                packedB.get() + (j / columnBlock) * N * columnBlock;
   205	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   206	#pragma omp simd
   207	                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < columnBlock; ++offset) {
   208	                    destination[k * columnBlock + offset] =
   209	                        b[k * N + j + offset];
   210	                }
   211	            }
   212	        }
   213	
   214	        // A cache-sized row slab remains hot while it visits all column
   215	        // panels.  Within a panel, several row micro-tiles reuse the same B
   216	        // data from private cache.  Threads own disjoint slabs, eliminating
   217	        // races and false sharing in the main multiplication.
   218	#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
   219	        for (size_t rowStart = 0; rowStart < fullRows;
   220	             rowStart += rowCacheBlock) {
   221	            const size_t rowEnd =
   222	                std::min(rowStart + rowCacheBlock, fullRows);
   223	            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
   224	                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
   225	                    multiplyFullTile(a, packedB.get(), c, N, i, j);
   226	                }
   227	            }
   228	            if (fullColumns < N) {
   229	                for (size_t i = rowStart; i < rowEnd; i += rowBlock) {
   230	                    multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, rowBlock,
   231	                                        fullColumns, N - fullColumns);
   232	                }
   233	            }
   234	        }
   235	
   236	        // At most three rows take this path.  Keeping them in the same team
   237	        // handles arbitrary matrix sizes without a serial cleanup phase.
   238	#pragma omp for schedule(static)
   239	        for (size_t i = fullRows; i < N; ++i) {
   240	            for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += columnBlock) {
   241	                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, j, columnBlock);
   242	            }
   243	            if (fullColumns < N) {
   244	                multiplyPartialTile(a, b, c, N, i, 1, fullColumns,
   245	                                    N - fullColumns);
   246	            }
   247	        }
   248	    }
   249	}

   251	// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
   252	bool validateResult(const double* const A, const double* const B,
   253	                    const double* const C, const size_t N) {
   254	    // Check a few random positions
   255	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   256	    
   257	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   258	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   259	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
   260	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
   261	            
   262	            double expected = 0.0;
   263	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   264	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   265	            }
   266	            
   267	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   268	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   269	            
   270	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   271	                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   272	                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   273	                return false;
   274	            }
   275	        }
   276	    }
   277	    
   278	    return true;
   279	}
   280	
   320	    const size_t elementCount = N * N;
   321	    auto A = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(elementCount);
   322	    auto B = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(elementCount);
   323	    auto C = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(elementCount);
   324	    
   325	    // Initialize matrices
   326	    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   327	    initMatrix(A.get(), N);
   328	    initMatrix(B.get(), N);
   329	    
   330	    // Perform matrix multiplication
   331	    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   332	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   333	    
   334	    matrixMultiply(A.get(), B.get(), C.get(), N);
   335	    
   336	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   337	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   338	    
   339	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   340	    
   341	    // Calculate GFLOPS
   342	    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
   343	    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   344	    
   345	    // Print results for external validation
   346	    if (printResults) {
   347	        std::vector<double> result;
   348	        if (elementCount != 0) {
   349	            result.assign(C.get(), C.get() + elementCount);
   350	        }
   351	        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
   352	    }
   353	    
   354	    // Validation
   355	    if (validate) {
   356	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   357	        bool valid = validateResult(A.get(), B.get(), C.get(), N);
   358	        
   359	        if (valid) {
   360	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   361	            return 0;
   362	        } else {
   363	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   364	            return 1;
   365	        }
   366	    }
   367	    
   368	    return 0;
   369	}

+inline void multiplyFullTile(const double* const A, const double* const B,
+                             double* const C, const size_t N,

+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    __m512d sum00 = _mm512_setzero_pd();

+        sum11 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        sum20 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        sum30 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm512_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+    }

+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 1) * N + column + 4, sum11);

+    _mm256_storeu_pd(C + (row + 3) * N + column + 4, sum31);

+    // SIMD is across independent columns, so it does not reassociate any dot

+            sums[2][j] += a2 * bValue;
+            sums[3][j] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }

+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBlock; ++i) {
